Text ne yashigo wayarsa, yana dubawa yaga company dayayi apply sun daukeshi aiki, cikin murna yafuto yazo zai fadawa yayansa wannan labarin 
Yana Zuwa falon yaga yana shiri yace" yaya lafiya naga kana shiri cikin sauri haka? "
Abba yace" hassan yayarka ce batajin dadi inaso nakaita asbiti ne,koda yaushe jikin nata ba dadi, "

" husna! Husna!! "


" Na'am Abba gani nan,"

 Ammu ne yace" to yaya kawo key din saina kaiku Gaba daya, "
Babu musu Abba yabashi key din motar suka tafi asbiti dukansu 
Gwajin farko doctor ya shaida musu mama tanada jaririn ciki na wata daya 

Da murna suka dawo gida bakin kowa yaqi rufuwa, anan kuma Ammu yasake fada musu labarin samun aikinsa awani company mota sabo da aka Bude a kano, ance ma wani balarabe ne ya budewa jikansa, haka sukai ta farin ciki ranar 




   **   ***   **



After 2 weeks, hannayensu sarke cikin na Juna, saudat na kuka feenah na kuka, saudat tace "yanzu feenah mun rabu kenan," feenah tace" haba saudat meyasa kike wannan mgnr, "saudat tace" Toshiknn yaushe zaki tafi zaria?"

Feenah tace" eto sai nanda 3days, "

saudat tace"ki qara sati daya kafin nan yaya yadawo daga masar, kakanmu yarikeshi yace sai yayi sati uku kafin nan yahuta zai dawo," feenah saita nunawa saudat tasan komai tace" ai zaizo zaria," saudat tace" nasani duk da haka 
Gashi zabe yana matsowa, "

feenah tace" to menene? "
Saudat ta kalleta da mamaki tace" wai kina nufin bakisan babanmu Dan siyasa bane?" Girgiza kai feenah tayi gabanta yana ci Gaba da faduwa, saudat tace" kiyi hakuri laifi nane daban sanar dakeba babanmu sanata ne, nasan kema da a kano kike dole zakisan yan siyasar Garin, yanzu kuma suna neman takarar deputy governor, "
hawaye feenah ta share tace" to Allah ya tabbatar da alkhairi sister", saudat rungume ta  tana kukan rabuwarsu, yayinda feenah take kukan bakin cikin abinda ahmad yayi Mata, haka suka rabu 

Tana cikin adaidaita tana goge hawaye, inda tasan ahmad Dan gidan Dan siyasa ne da bata saurareshiba, domin kuwa tasan yafi karfin ta, yagama cutar ta yatafi wata qasa wai yana hutawa hawayen idonta ta goge aranta tace nayi DANA SANI ni nafisa

Dedicated to my family 



23



Tahir yace "haba ahmad, meyasa kake neman sauya halinka ne? Bahaka nasan ka ba ahmad, pls kadauki kaddara mai kyau ko marar kyau wannan shine abinda manzonmu yace" 

shiru kawai ahmad yayi 
Saida tahir ya tabbatar yabashi kulawar data dace sannan yadauki lemon yatafi gida 
Tashin motar sa ne ya farkar da mamy hakima daga bacci, Amma sai tayi zatan ko ahmad ne yadawo, tayi juyi taci Gaba da bacci 



   **   ***   **



Awannan daren ahmad bai runtsa ba, kawai hango kanshi yake yana Dakar wanda yayi masa wannan aikin, sai gab da a suba sannan bacci ya daukeshi gamida zazzabi mai zafi, hakance ta faru awajan tahir ma, dakyar yasamu baccin ya daukeshi 




   **   ***   **




Karfe 6:48 natashi, kafafuna na ziro kasa, ba laifi jikin nawa da sauqi, banajin komai saide abinda baza'a rasaba, falo na futo anan naga kaka tana lazimi, tace" sannu nafi, da naga bakya Jin dadi shiyasa ban tashekiba, "

nace" aikuwa kaka yanzu ma baccin bai isheni ba,"

 tace" sannu, aide da sauqi ko? "

Nace" kaka da sauqi sosai ai jiyan an yimin allura nasan shiyasa naji dama dama, "

tace" to sannu, Allah yasawaqe "

nace" amin, "

nayi "hammam" (toilet) 
Bayan na idar da sallah awajan bacci yaqara fizgata dakyar de natashi nakoma gado, naci Gaba da bacci, saide har yanzu bandaina Jin wani Abu a maqogaro naba, hakan kuma baya rasa nasaba da bala'in dana shiga jiya 




   **   ***   **




Da break suna zaune kowa yanacin abincin sa, shiru shiru suna neman tashi basuga ahmad ba, mamy hakima tace "nifa banga Dan gidanaba, kode har yanzu murnar company din ce, jiya ma ina bacci naji shigowar motarsa," 

auta maryam datasa indomie dinta agaba tanaci tadago tace

 "laaa ai yaya tahir ne yatafi, ba yaya ne ba,"

 kowa yajuyo yazuba Mata ido cikin mamaki, 
Tace" nima jiya inata jiran yaya yazo naga baizo ba shine naqi bacci, saida naganshi shida yaya tahir, wai yayama ba shida lafiya "

Momy ce tace" auta"
 cikin mamaki 
Mabaruka da basa ga maciji tace "kuma Dan iskanci kiyiwa mutane magana shine zaki zauna kina ma cin abinci ko Ajikin ki?" 
Cikin shagwaba maryam tace" to ina ruwanki, Momy kin ganta ko," tafada tana kallan momyn ahmad, 
Momy tace" yi shiru auta, kyaleta keda yaya ai mutu ka raba"

mabaruka harara ta zabga Mata ta tashi tayi part din ahmad, 
Akan gado ta ganshi ya takure kansa yana bacci, qarasawa tayi ta yayeshi, wani zafi ya bugota, tace          " subhanallah, yaya, yaya," 
dakyar ya Bude idonsa tace" ashe bakada lafiya, sannu,"
 gyada kansa yayi kawai 
Ruwa ta hada masa na wanka mai sauqin zafi, tatashe shi yaje yayi wanka, yana shiga wankan ta gyara Dakin sannan tafita 

Komawa tayi ta sanar dasu halin da ake ciki, aikuwa dukansu suka diba yuuuu suka tafi ganinsa, ita kuma taci Gaba da cin abincin ta 

Yana zaune Akan gado harya futo, suka zo suka zagayeshi, idan kagansu anan ne zaka tabbatar gidan akwai kwanciyar hankali da hadin kai 
Saudat, Muslim, bukhari, Momy, mamy hakima, yayinda auta ta dane jikinsa, bata damu da zafin da jikin nasa yake yiba 
Momy ce tace "waladeey ya jikin Naka? Meyasa bazaka kira waya kasanar mana ba?" 

"Momy ai ban kwanta da zazzabin ba, kuma yanzu ma da sauqi," 
mamy hakima tace" sannu my son, "

Yace" yauwa mamy,"

" bani wayarka, "

dauka yayi yabata, ta Nemo number tahir 
Yana dagawa yace" ahmad ya jikin Naka?"

 Mamy hakima tace" pls tahir, zoka duba shi yanadan Jin zazzabi kadan, "

doctor tahir yace" to mamy gani nan insha Allah,"

takashe wayar 

Mabaruka ce tadawo Dakin cikin shirin school tace to saiku tashi mu tafi ai, bukhari da Muslim ne suka tashi, auta ko motsi batayiba, Muslim yace" toki tashi mana,"
 tace" nide bazaniba tunda yaya ba shida lafiya, "

saudat ce tace" a a auta, karma ki fara wannan rikicin danba me bullewa bane, oya tashi, "

aikuwa yarinya tafashe da kuka, ahmad ne yace " auta naji sauqi fa, idan kika dawo zamuyi wasa da jirgi da mota, "tace" to yaya Amma saide in Zata goyeni," saudat tace" wa zai goyeki yarinya, garama ki warke," maryam tace" Allah saikin goyani," ba yanda saudat ta iya haka ta goyeta saida ta kaita har mota sannan tajuyo 



   **   ***   **



" Salamu Alaikum,"

kaka ta dinga godiya ga surukan nata, mutanan kwarai 




   **   ***   **




Gumi ne yagama wanke masa fuska ganin irin illar da maganin yake dashi, sosai ansa maganin dayawa acikin just din, kuma ko waye yayi hakan da mugunta, domin kuwa Babu marar imanin dazai aika ta haka, dama yasan wannan al'amarin akwai sa hannun wani aciki, sannan bayan  haka qwayar tanada hatsari sosai, Amma bazai tabbatar da shin ahmad din yasamu abinda yake tunanin ba, saide idan yayi aure ne zai tabbatar, zubar da lemon yayi,  Yabar asbitin yayi gidansu ahmad din 




   **   ***   **




Duk suna zaune inda suke da, saide saudat da hand out ahannunta tana dubawa, tahir yayi sallama yashigo, shi kanshi sun bashi tausayi, gaskya suna kaunar ahmad dayawa, wai nan zazzabi ma kenan an zauna anyi jugum inaga sun San gaskiyar abinda yafaru kuma. 

Abinci yagama ci dama, Dan haka yabashi magunguna da qarin ruwa,sannan su Momy suka fita suka barshi ya huta, 
Ahmad ya kalli tahir yace" kai Malam nifa na warke kawai idan na tuna abinda yafaru ne banajin duniyar tanamin dadi "

Tahir yace" dole kacire abin nan aranka, kar hawan jini ya kamaka,"

 ahmad yace" hakane insha Allah zan kiyaye dahiru, "

harararsa tahir yayi 
Ahmad yace" toba dahirun bane, dahirun ummi "
Tahir kallansa yayi, yana murmushi yace" nawan har naji dadi wallahi daka warware "
Ahmad yace" toya zanyi tahir Allah yadoramin abinda dole saide inyi hakuri in karbi kaddarata, Amma kuma fa yarinyar tayi, tayi, ba karya akwai zaqi, 🙈wallahi tana kukan nan banma San me take fadaba, "

tahir ne yakai masa duka, yace" Allah mutumina,"

 ahmad yana murmushi gamida hawaye yace" serious tahir, gaskya yanayin akwai dadi "

Tahir yace" muma de zamu dage mu yi auren muji yaya akeji, "

ahmad yace" gaskya kam, nima zan nemi yafiyar nafeesa ne kawai inta amince ayi mana auren mu, "

tahir yace" why not"

Ahmad yace "yauwa ya mgnr abin nan," 
anan tahir yafada masa komai ahmad yace" acikin gidan nan ina zaune ake wannan qulla qullar lalle kuwa zan bincika, akwai rikici "

Tahir yace" kaide kabi komai a sannu insha Allah komai zai warware, "

" Hakane, "
inji ahmad 

" Yawwaa Tahir ya maganar babyn ka din nan, dakake tunani, "

Tahir yace" ina tunani ahmad kana jinya, so nake ka warke saina  kirata infada Mata "

Ahmad yace" no kakira ta Yau inka koma gida, ka tsarata sosai yanda bazata qi amincewa ba,"

" Toshiknn" inji Tahir 

Ahmad wayarsa ya janyo gabansa na faduwa, yayi dialing number feenah, gara yakirata inma zaginsa zatayi kawai tayi inzata huce 
Saide kuma wayar akashe, dole saide idan ruwan yakare yaje ya nemeta agida, Allah sarki rayuwa su kuma haka tasu kaddarar take 
Wayar ya cillar gefe, Tahir yayi masa allurar bacci yace yakamata ya huta, shikuma yakoma bakin aikinsa 

Saudat ce tanata shiri Zata je wajan feenah, tunda batazo Dan ziyara ba aitazo Dan jinyar yaya, Momy ce tashigo Dakin tace "kekuma ina zaki," 

saudat tace "Momy nayi ta Kiran wayar sister feenah ne, inasan magana da ita Amma wayar akashe shine nakeso inje inga ko lafiya," 

Momy tace "saudat rigima, banda abinki ai jibi zaku shiga exam 
Dole zaku hadu, kiyi hakuri nasan insha Allah tana lafiya, kiyi karatu dakyau, idan Baku haduba Zuwa jibin daga school dinma saiki wuce, Amma yanzu de kije ki kula da brother dinki," 

"Toshiknn Momy" 



 
   **   ***   **




Bayan kwana biyu ahmad ya warke sosai, saide Gaba day ba shida walwala, Gaba dayansa ya canja, shiru shiru Babu magana, yayinda feenah sun Dade da fara exam, inda tabawa saudat uzurin cewa batada lafiya ne, shiyasa kuma ga exam agabansu ta karshe shiyasa ta kashe Wayarta, haka saudat ta hakura badan ranta ya soba, a bangaren feenah kuwa ita kanta saudat din haushinta takeji 🙊🤣


Ahmad yashiga Hali na wahala kullum cikin tunani ga tafiyarsa Cairo na matsowa ga shi yana so ya gyara tsakanin sa da feenah, Amma taqi bashi damar hakan 
Kullum saiyaje kofar gidansu, haka kullum sai ance masa bata nan, kaka tayi tambayar duniya Akan su waye suke kiranta tace kawai samarin unguwa ne, wanda ita feenah tasan ahmad ne, 
Akwai lokacin da kaka tace Mata taje ta duba ko yayan saudat ne, Amma qememe feenah tace ai suna waya dashi bashi bane haka kaka ta hakura 



   **   ***   **



Gidan yashigo ransa abace yayi part din Momy tadaga kai ta kalleshi tace "waladeey lafiya kuwa?" 
Yace "Momy lokacin danayi rashin lafiyar nan waye yashiga part dinane?" Tace "gaskya Babu, kasan saudat tana karatun exam so bata shiga mabaruka kuma kasan dama akwai kasala saudat tafi ta gyaran, bana tunanin tashiga," 

"OK" kawai yace yatashi yafuta fuuuu 
Momy tabishi da kallan mamaki, tome aka masa? 

Main falo yadawo yana kwalawa walaya kira," walaya!  Walaya!! Walaya!!!" 
Yasan cewa tana aiki afalo sosai may be zatasan wayashiga 

Kai tsaye yace "Mata wakika ga yashiga part dina kwanaki acikin yaran gidan nan?" 
Lokaci daya ta firgita da Jin furucin sa, har ahmad yaso yayi tunanin wani Abu, cikin duburcewa tace "wal..... Walla... Wallahi bansaniba ba," baice komai ba yajuya yayi wajan baba maigadi, ai walaya tana ganin yatafi wajan baba maigadi damm gabanta yayi wani irin faduwa, nan take ta dora hannun ta aka 🙆🏻

Tana shiga gida Dakin ta ta wuce ta dinga kuka, Bama wannan ne abin damuwar ba, yanda takasa cireshi acikin ranta, gaskiya so masifa ne, ta tabbata yanzu shi yana cikin kwanciyar hankali, kaka ce take Jin sauti kamar na kuka, ta futo, tana daga labulan Dakin tace "ah nafi ashe kin dawo, menene kuma abin kuka?
Dama rabuwa da abokai ai tazama dole, nima haka zaki tafi ki barni nafi nasaba dake agidan nan" kaka takarashe maganar tana kuka 
Haka suka rungume Juna suna kuka itada kaka Babu mai rarrashin wani 

Kwanana uku a kano, nayi shirin tafiya zaria, banajin kuma zan qara waiwaitar wannan Garin, danma na taya kaka hada kayanta dazata koma gidansu aysha 



     **   ***   **

nace" ai nafada masa na amince, "daria suka saki Gaba dayansu suka tafa

"Feenah, feenah, tashi kiyi sallah mana," 

"kai anty husna ke akwai damu,"

baki naturo Gaba, na tashi nayi toilet 

Bayan sati daya, su husna sun Gama school, haka soyaiyar su da doctor sai abinda yaqaru koda yaushe suna cikin waya, suna kaunar junansu sosai harma da alkawarin aure atsakani 



   **   ***  **

   **   ***   **

Daga murya yayi yana kiranta, ta futo "
Yaya gani," 

wayarsa ya miqa Mata, tace "yaya bangane ba," 
Yace "sakamin Num feenah, kokuma number da kikasan zan same ta idan naje zaria" 

Saudat tace "wallahi yaya bansaniba," 

mamaki shinfide kan fuskar sa yace "bangane ba, kamarya baki sani ba?" 
Tace "wlh yaya nima kaina nema nake," 
yace "toko zamuje zaria din mu dubata," 

saudat tace "yaya to zaria ina? 
Ina zamu nufa? Sannan ko jiya nadau mota naje gidan kaka an tabbatar min tariga da tatashi agidan, ni maganar bikinama nakeso nasanar Mata," 

alama yamata da hannu Akan tatafi, karta qara masa wata damuwar 😃




   **   ***   **



"Mama ga maganin naki fa da doctor yabayar nasan baki shaba," 

"wlh husna banasan maganin qarin jinin nan banajin dadinsa," 
"to mama kiyi hakuri kisha," 

"to bani nasha, tashi kiramin autata tazo ta dora abinci," 

"yanzu mama saura 7 months ki haihu,Amma kina ce Mata auta," 

mama tace "husna gidanku, kunji yarinya ke har wani lissafi ma kike, tashi kiramin ita" 

Daki tashiga zaton ta, karatun novel nake, Amma sai taga sabanin haka, bacci na nake shararawa haiqam, kallo na tayi da mamaki, aranta tace Tome yake damun yarinyar nan, tashi na tashiga yi, dakyar na tashi, ina tashi na shaqi qamshin turaren ta, aikuwa da gudu nayi toilet na dinga shara amai, husna tabini da kallo gabanta yana faduwa 
Ina futowa tabini da kallo, nace "sister wannan turaren naki bana sansa, Dan Allah fita daga Dakin nan," 

husna tace "yar renin wayo dagani har ke dashi muke amfani kafin kitafi school...... 

Kafin tagama magana nasake yin toilet da gudu nayi wani aman, aikuwa ina futowa tajani, ta duba idona, takama hannu na, ta duba, cikin loka ta dauko pt strip, tace" jeki iyo min fitsari anan"

 tabata wata roba, aikuwa ba musu feenah taje tayi, jikinta duk yayi sanyi, ta futo tabawa husna
Jikin husna ne yadauki rawa ganin layi guda biyu Ajikin pt din, wato kenan abinda take tunani ne yake San tabbata, lokaci daya hawaye yasilalo daga idanunta tana hawaye tace "feenah"

Ganin yaqi bata wayar yasa tasa hannu ta karba, "hello yata yagida?" 
Husna cikin ladabi tace "mama ina wuni?" 
Ummi cikin Jin dadi ta nemi wajan zama tace "alhmdlh ummin tahir ce" 
Husna zare ido tayi tace "ummi ina yini ai ban ganekiba" 
Ummi Ana murmushi a kace  "a a ai ba komai yarinya, yaya sunan kine?" 
Tace "husna" 
Ummi tace "masha Allah, yaushe zakizo naganki?" 

Husna tace "ai da nisa, saide wani lkcin idan munzo kano sai nazo" 
Ummi tace "a a ba'a nan Garin kikeba?" 
Husna tace  "eh wlh muna zaria"

"to Ummi na gode sai anjima," 
ta kashe wayar

Tace "ita kuma wannan fa?" 
Tahir yayi qasa da kansa yace "Ummi dalibata ce" 
Ummi tace "um aikam nagani, yanzu ma ai naga karatu kuke, idan kun Gama karatun ne zaku samu yan biyun" 🤣 

Tahir yace cikin shagwaba "kai Ummi nifa bance haka ba" 
Ummi cikin Jin dadi tace "to koma de menene ka shirya kuje ku gana, idan da Hali ma ayi komai agama cikin wata daya," zaro ido yayi yana kallanta, tace" to menene? 
Bakasan yanda na matsu kayi aure bane tahir shiyasa, kai kadai gareni 
Banda kowa, mahaifinka ya rasu, koso kake nima in mutu banga auren kaba? "
Cikin sauri tahir ya girgiza kansa yace" a a Ummi insha Allah zanje cikin satin nan "
Ummi ta shafa kansa tace" yauwa Dan autana "



   **   ***   **



After 3days

Tahir yayi murmushi yace" banda qanne banda yaiyu, ni kadai Ummi ta haifa, kafin daga baya Allah yadauki ran mahaifina, itace ta tsaya wajan kula da komai nasa har na mallaki hankalin kaina, har Zuwa yanzu "

Tace "shiyasa kake shagwaba," 

daria tabawa tahir, yace "yanzu ahakan inada shagwaba?" 
Tace "sosai ma," 
yace "lalle Dan bakiga dan'uwana bane," 
tace "waye hakan?" 
Yace "aminina ahmad, shi agidansu shine babba, Amma idan ya kwaba miki wani abin, saiki dauka shine auta" 
Husna tayi daria tace " lalle, nima kuwa Dan bakasan autar mama bane, Babu abinda tabari, Amma ganin batada qani ne shiyasa, 
Kai karshe ma rikicin ta ne yasa Ammu yace akaita boarding, kwata kwata bataji" 
Tahir yace "um kaji manya wato ke babba ko?" 
Husna tace "bakasan rikicin yarinyar nan bane, idan na share ta saita rainani" 
Tahir yace "um kaji manya," husna cikin shagwaba tace "Allah my one zan kashe," cikin sauri yace "no yi hakuri nadai na, shikkenan?
Yanzu fadamin yaushe zanzo?" 
Tace "duk lkcin daka samu dama," yace "toshkn zaki ganni jibi insha Allah" tace "to Allah yakaimu" 
Yace" to yanzu fadamin abinda zanji dadi indinga tunashi har inyi bacci "
Husna tace" to rufe idonka"

 

   **   ***   **


Yayi neman number ta harya gaji 
Gaba daya yarasa me yake masa dadi 
Daga karshe ya saduda yamiqa lamuransa ga Allah, yasan Babu wani bawa da zai kaucewa qaddararsa, abinda yasa aransa dayane shine kawai shida qara kula wata mace da sunan soyaiya har abada 
Itama azal ce ta fada masa harya furta Mata kauna, Dan tunda yake shide ba ma'abocin kula Mata bane, saide ma su soshi, zai sawa ransa dangana, yayi addu'ah Allah yakawo masa karshan wannan matsala 

Gaba daya yazama shiru shiru, dama yaya aka qare 
Kamar kullum yauma yana kwance adakinsa yazuba wa P. O. P din Dakin ido 
Tayi sallama ta shigo, tashi yayi zaune yace" momy kece? "

Tace" waladeey, menene yake damunka? "
Ahmad yace" Babu komai momy kawai aiyukane sukai yawa" 
Tace "Ahmad bakada wata mahaifiya bayan ni, bansan meyasa kake San boyemin damuwarka ba, ko kanaga kanada wadda tafini?" 
Cikin sauri ya girgiza kansa, nanda nan idanunsa suka ciko 
Momy janyo shi tayi jikinta ta dora kansa Akan cinyarta 
Tace "yi hakuri to, menene abin kuka kuma, yi shiru kaji waladeey na" 
Haka tayi ta rarrashinsa daga karshe tace yadaure yafada Mata abinda yake damunsa, tiryan tiryan yafada Mata budurwarsa ce tunda tagama karatu har Yau ya nemi number ta yarasa, kuma baisan gidansu ba, 
Momy jikinta yayi sanyi tasan tunda ahmad yashiga damuwa Akan Abu to tabbas ya nasan abun sosai 

Momy ta nisa tace" ahmad kayi hakuri mana, kamiqa lamuranka ga Allah insha Allah zakaga abinda zai biyo baya ka dinga addu'ah Allah yabaiyana mana ita "
Kasa magana yayi saide kawai ya daga kai alamun to 

Kansa taci Gaba da shafawa har taji jikinsa yasaki alamun yayi bacci sannan ta gyara masa kwanciya ta fita 



   **   ***   **


Gaye kayi sauri fa, nasan halinka, ahmad cikin kasala yace" dallah Malam ni majima da shiryawa zakaje gidan surukai kamar zakaje wata qasa "

Tahir yace" eh naji, futo nazo kofar gidan zan daukeka "
Futowa yayi cikin baqin yadi da baqar hula yasa agogo shima baqi, da takalmi shima de baqin, kalar fatarsa saita qara futowa fara tas acikin kayan duk da yanada Dan duhu kadan, Amma mai karatu kaso kaga ahmad a wannan kayan 

Su momy suna falo, yazo ya tsugunna har qasa ya gaida su, mamy hakima tace "dana duk wannan kwalliyar ta office dince?" 
Shide kawai murmushi yayi ya miqe yace musu saina dawo 

Momy kawai da ido ta bishi Dan tasan abinda yake damun Dan nata 

Yana fita ya ganshi acikin motarsa fara, shiga yayi yace" muje,  "tahir yace" abokina irin wannan kwalliya haka kamar kaine saurayin? "🤣 
Wannan ai saikasa husnan ta dawo wajanka" 

Ahmad yace "kaga Malam Dan Allah muje haba, sunyi nisa sosai wayar ahmad tadau qara, yana dubawa yaga hassan ne, ya daga can daya bangaren Ammu yace" masa" yallabai baqin turawannan sunzo, wanda kace idan sun zo ayima magana zaka gana dasu, ahmad yace" ya Salam wallahi na manta gani nan Zuwa," bayani ya yiwa tahir, badan yasoba haka yasaukeshi, ahmad ya leqo ta glass din motar yace saika dawo abokina dama ni wannan tafiyar wallahi gabana faduwa yake," tahir kawai harara ya miqa masa yaja yatafi, daga baya ahmad yayi waya company su azo a daukeshi

" Abban husna yaufa muna da baqi, "

d mamaki yace" haba?" Anan ta dinga bashi labarin feenah har yaji yanasan ganinta, suna tsaye Abba yadawo daga unguwa 

Tambayoyi ya yiwa tahir Babu bata lokaci ya amsa, Abba ya yaba da hankalin sa, gudin kada azo ayi DANA SANI irin na nafeesa 🤣shiyasa Abba yace yabashi dama ya turo magabatansa ayi maganar bikinsu, duk wani bincike daya kamata ayi, zaiyi insha Allah

Ihu nasa da qarfi ina fadin "na shiga uku na, Abba Dan Allah kayafemin wallahi ba laifi na bane," 

mama ce tatashi tace. "gaskya alhaji Babu inda Zata, yaya baka tsaya kaji abinda yafaru ba zaka yanke hukunci cikin fishi," 

husna ganin iyayenta zasu fara samun sabani abinda basu taba ganiba tunda suke, nan itama takama kuka, tana rokon mahaifinsu Akan Dan Allah yayi hakuri 
Abinda yabasu mamaki shine yanda shi kansa Abban su kaga yana hawaye 

Mama ce tasauko tafara bashi hakuri, yace "to shikkenan tunda bazata barmin gidan ba ni bari in bar muku gidan" 

Mama ce tayi sauri ta riko kafarsa, tace "Abban husna Dan girman Allah kayi hakuri," 

tana kuka tana bashi hakuri, tace "yanzu yaran nan su biyu garemu, meyasa kake so su fuskanci matsala arayuwarsu? 
Karfa ka manta Amana ce Allah yabamu tasu" 

Abba yace "haba me zaku fadamin, ina zaune agidan kunsan abinda yake faruwa Amma kukasa sanar Dani, to so kuke saita haihu sannan zaku sanar Dani?" 

Mama tace "kayi hakuri alhaji munsan munyi laifi, Amma kayi hakuri ka fuskanci abinda zan sanar dakai 

Mama bata boye masaba ta bashi labarin komai, idanunsa yayi jajir 

Yadago yace" uwa ta kin tabbatar haka abin yake? "
Ina kuka nace" Allah Abba hakane, wallahi fyade yayimin, Allah bahalina bane hakan "
Yace" to shikkenan kuskure de anriga da anyi 
Hakika kuma nayi DANA SANI Akan zuwanki kano ma baki daya 
DANA SANI lokacin da yaron nan auwalu yanuna yana sanki dana baki shi kunje can kun qarata, nayi DANA SANI nafeesa, "

dago kai nayi ina kallansa Jin sunan daya kirani sunan mahaifiyarsa Amma tashin hankali yahanashi boyewa

 nace" Abba wallahi bansan inda yake ba,"

Yace  " kinyi kuskure uwa ta, shikuma duk daren dadewa zaizo ya sameni, insha Allah sai yayi DANA SANI"

sun nutsu kowa yana cin abinci Amma Gaba daya hankalin sa bayama kan abincin

ta tashi itama tabi bayansa

Yana kwance awata "hadiqa" (wajan shan iska) 
Ya lumshe idonsa, Zuwa tayi  kwanta ajikinsa, yana Jin qamshin turaren ta, ya tabbatar itace ta qara so, hannu yasa yana shafa gadon bayanta 

Idanunsa a lumshe Yace "haba my feenah meyasa zaki gujeni?" 

Dora kaina nayi akirjinsa, ina shafa sumar kansa nace "my ahmad ina tare dakai a koda yaushe, kaima kasan bazan iya qinka ba, ahmad ina sanka," 
Bude idonsa yayi yayi tozali da kyakykyawar fuskarta, Dan lips dinta yasha pink din Jan baki, yayi kyau, tana magana ya Zuba Mata idanu, iska ta hura masa a fuska tace" haba hammad wannan kallanfa? "

Baice komai ba yahade bakinsu waje daya 
Kokarin kwace wa take, dakyar de ta kwace tafara gudu yana binta sunata gudu suna dariya, tajuyo tayi masa gwalo" yeeee yakasa kamani" 

Juya war da zatayi kawai ta afka cikin rami, da gudu yakaraso yace  " my feeeeenahhhh!!!" 

Azabure ya tashi daga baccin dayake, Gaba daya yahada wani uban gumi, Dakin ya kalla da esi da komai rigarsa ya cire Yabar best din ciki 

Sumar kansa yashafa, ya kalli agogon dake ajiye a drowar gefen gadon karfe hudu na yamma, da sauri yatashi domin yagabatar da sallar la'asar

"to Abban husna ina jinka"

Yace "sai kuma magana ta biyu, Akan maganar uwata, nace saboda mutane masu shigowa meze hana Takoma can kano da zama gidan mama, kafin Allah yasauketa lafiya sai asan ya za'a yi, tunda kinga bazai iyu mu qara daukar wani zunubin ba wajan zubar da cikin kokuwa ya kikace "

Mama tace" gaskiya alhaji Babu abinda zamu ce dakai sai godia hakan ma yayi idan ta zauna anan muna tare ni na haihu itama ta haihu Babu dadi, "Abba yace" nima shine abinda nagani

Wani dumm takeji akanta, Gaba daya ganinta yake neman daukewa hakan ya haddasa Mata zama aqasa dabas 
Cikin daki kuwa kuka na dinga yi hannu na yanakan kaina, ina na. "shiga uku na, Ahmad ka ha'inceni," 

husna ce ta zabga min mari, tace "zakimana shiru kosai na qara miki?" 

Amma ina kojinta ba nayi kuka na nake haiqam, tace "nacemiki uban wa ya miki ciki, tun muna yara nasan wannan ba halinki bane, meyasa zuwanki makaranta kika sauya daga yanda muka sanki nafeesa,"

 Jin Kiran sunana datayi kanta tsaye, shiya tabbatar min da lalle ran husna ya baci 

Ban boye Mata komai ba, ina kuka nafada Mata, tana kuka itanma ta rungume ni kamar masu zaman makoki muna kuka, tace" ki shirya kawai gobe muje gidansu"
 
Nace Mata "bansan gidan ba" 

Sake wani kukan tayi, mu kadai adaki muna koke koke, husna ce ta tuna da Aiken da mama tayi Mata, ta miqe da sauri tayo falo, kanta ne yayi wani mugun sarawa ganin mama datayi a zaune a kofar dakinsu, tana kallan waje daya kamar mutum mutumi, saurin goge idonta tayi tace "mama bari indora girkin autar bacci take," 

tayi hanyar waje, kallanta mama take, wato so suke su biyu Susan maganar ba tare da I yayansu sun saniba, basasan asirin daya daga cikin su ya tonu, tashi mama tayi ko magana bata yi Mata ba, tashige Dakin 
Ina zaune abakin gado ina goge hawaye tare da tsinewa ahmad🤣 mama tashigo ta sameni 

Tace "auta" 

dagowa nayi cike da tsoro qarara Akan fuska ta, tace "ki fadamin tsakanin ki da Allah abinda naji haka yake" 

kuka nafashe dashi nace  " mama Dan girman Allah ki yafe min, wallahi mama ba laifi na bane Dan Allah mama ki rufamin asiri, wallahi mama badan duk wanda ya kashe kansa yamutu Dan wuta ba, wallahi mama dana kashe kaina da wannan abin kunyar, nayi DANA SANIN zuwana kano ma Gaba daya mama, "

Mama kasa hakuri tayi, ta rungume ni tana kuka, husna ma datake tsaye abakin kofa tashigo mama duka ta rungume mu muna kuka, tace" auta kiyi hkr, haka Allah mukuma ya jarrabemu da wannan masifa, "

tafada tana kuka kamar ba mama ba 

Mike wa tayi tace" husna ku dauko mayafinku mu fita, "

Babu musu muka tashi, itama taje daki ta futo cikin hijab dinta 
Waje muka fita, muka tari taxi, bamu zarce ko ina ba, sai wani private hospital, bamu dadeba muka nemi ganin likita, aka nuna mana office dinsa, zamu shige Gaba dayanmu wani yace mana" bayin Allah mutum dai dai ake shiga,"

 mama tajuyo tace" dukanmu lalurar dayace,"🤣 

mukasa kai ciki 
Kujerar mama ce ta zauna, doctor ya kalleta da mamaki yace" mama yanaga ku uku,"

 tace" kayi hkr dana "

Anan mama tayi masa bayanin tana so acire cikin dayake jikina, ko nawane yafada Mata sai su bashi kudin, likitan ya kalleta da mamaki 
Yace" mama ni musulmi ne kema kuma nasan hakance, meyasa kun aikata laifi abaya kukesan qara wani laifin? "

Mama tace" yaro ka taimaka mana, bansan halin dazamu fuskanta ba daga wajan mahaifinsu dagani harsu,"

likita yace" mama kiyi hakuri gaskya bana wannan aikin 

Kema ina baki shawara kiyi hakuri, kuje kuyi masa bayani idan bai yarda ba, ki sanar Dani nayi miki alkawari zan auri yarinyar ki ahaka, koda kuwa ata biyu ne,🤣"

 mama tace" to yaro Mungo de "

Muka futo dukanmu jiki asanyaye, 
Gida muka koma direct anan muka ga Abba yadawo yana zaune afalo, gabana ne yayi mugun faduwa, ganina shima zai gane halin danake ciki, yace" maman nafeesa ina ku kaje ne? 
Tun dazu na dawo Gaba dayanku bakwa nan gida Babu kowa gashi ko abinci ma Babu,"

 mama tace" ayi hakuri Abban husna yanzu za'a dora,"
 tafada tana cire hijabin jikinta, nida husna muka zauna ahannun kujera kamar wasu baqi 
Abba yakalllemu yace

" kukuma miye haka kamar wasu yara, Yau Babu hatsaniyar da kuka saba kenan? "🤣 


Mama tayi karaf tace" to alhaji munje gidan gaisuwa ai dole jikinsu yayi sanyi, can bayane wata baquwa datazo ziyara, ta haihu danta ya mutu shine take kuka abin tausayi shine suma nan kagansu haka🤣😳"

Idanu muka hada nida husna, lalle iyaye Mata sunyi a rayuwa, Abba ne yace" kajiku da shirme to mutuwa ai tana kan kowa 
Ke auta zonan ki hadamin wannan takardun,"

 jikina yana rawa nace" to Abba,"
 nakarba ina shiryasu

Biki biki biki 
Awannan nan lokacin anata hidimar bikin Ammu, kowa yana busy, musanman mama, saide duk ta rame, wannan Abu na nafeesa yana nukurkusarta, Gaba daya batada nutsuwa, ta kasa sanar da mahaifinsu, bangaren feenah kuwa, sai abinda yakaru Dan ciki yana Nan yana qara girma abinsa, watansa biyu yanzu yayinda na mama yake wata uku

da sauri

Abba yace" to mahaifiyata, ina fada kina fada,😃 "
yayi Gaba 
Mama tasan fishi yayi shiyasa yafadi haka 
Dan haka taja hijab dinta ta rike feenah sukai asbiti tare da Abba 




   **   ***   **




Acan kano kuwa iyayan amarya maimuna sunata hidimar biki suma, daidai misali komai yana tafiya yanda yakamata 
Zuwa dare kuma amarya Zata tafi zaria gidan mijin ta 




   **   ***   ** 



"Am likita kayimin bayani yanda zan gane, wannan yarinyar na kawo ma ba maman ta ba, 🤣maman ta itace mai Juna biyu," 

doctor Bala ya cire glass din idonsa, yace "alhaji haka bincikemu ya nuna, batada wani zazzabi ko malaria, munyi Mata text abinda sakamako ya nuna kenan" 
Abba Gaba daya ya nemi nutsuwarsa yarasa duk jikinsa rawa yake, yajuyo yace "mamana," 
kawai yaga ina share hawaye 

Yajuyo ya kalli mama yaga takasa hada ido dashi, kenan abinda likita yafada gaskiya ne, 
Awannan rana dayake farin ciki zai aurar da qaninsa mafi soyuwa aransa Amma wannan yarinyar ta dauko masa wannan abin kunyar 

Tashi yayi yana kade binjimar jikinsa yana fifita da ita ya cewa doctor "toshkn likita na gode sosai," 

yayi waje cikin sauri 
Su mama ma sum sum suka bishi abaya 

Amota Babu abinda kakeji sai sautin kukana, nasan tunda Abba yayi shiru tabbas akwai abinda yake shiryawa 

Mama kanta tama kasa kallansa bare ni 

Har muka zo gida Babu wanda ya cewa uffan acikin mu, muna Zuwa nafice daga motar da gudu nayi cikin gida, Abba yafuto ya kalli mama yace Mata "kin kyauta" 

shima yayi hanyar masallaci 



   **   ***   **



Bayan sati daya, amarya ta tare agidan ta, suna zaune lafiya, Amma ta bangare na, Babu wannan lafiyar, Gaba daya Abba fishi yake damu

Ko gaishe shi nayi baya amsawa haka zan qaraci zamana Natashi, ta bangaren mama kuwa har gara nima 
Ita kwata kwata bata ganin fuskar gaisuwar ma 
Na rame nakara lalacewa 

Cikin jikinane kawai yakesa aga kumari na 

Washe gari Abba yana zaune afalo, yakira mama bayan tazo yace taje takiramu Gaba daya 

Gabana yana faduwa naziro hijabi na na futo kaina aqasa 
Husna ma tana Bina abaya muka zauna aqasa kanmu a sunkuye 

Abba yace "Nafeesa" 

Gabana yayi mugun faduwa yace "kafin nace dake komai nafeesa tashi ki dauko min jakar ki" 

Mama ta kalle shi da mamaki me zaiyi da jakarta? 
Daki nakoma ina kuka ina hada kayana, kusan kala biyar nadauko na futo, na tsugunna agabansu ina goge hawaye 

Hannu yasa a aljihu ya dauko kudi dubu biyu yabani, nakarba 

Yace "yauwa kama hanya kifice ki barmin gidana," 

mama batasan lokacin data tashi ba, tace "alhaji kamar yaya? 
Ina kake so taje mace cefa," Abba yace "taje koma inane, nide bazata haifamin shege agidanaba, wallahi nayi tirr da halinki nafeesa nayi DANA SANI Akan karatun dana turaki kano, maza fice ki barmin gidana.

"Gani mama," 

"to autar mama, kinji de abinda Abban ku yace, kuma ni aganina hakan ai yafi ko auta?" 
Nace "hakane mama, yanzu yaushe zamu tafi?" 

"A a kibar husna anan taringa tayani wasu abubuwan kokuwa?

"hakane mama Toshiknn bari inje in shirya kayan" 



   **   ***   **



"Mama wallahi kinji de abinda yafaru," 

kaka cikin kuka tace "Amma ma'azu Baku kyauta minba, yanzu yarinyar mu zauna tare da ita Tsawon lokaci, wannan Abu yafaru bazaku sanar daniba, haba ma'azu," 
Abba yace "mama kiyi hakuri, wallahi abin ne ba shida dadin fadi, nasan kuma hankalin ki zai tashi Akan hakan,

"Gobe Zata taho insha Allah" 

"To Allah yakawo ta lafiya" 

"Amin mama" 

Bangaren qanwar mama ta nufa, tana kuka ta sanar da ita komai, haka kalatynmu itama tahau kuka abin, gwanin ban tausayi

"Salamu Alaikum," 

"wa'alaikumussalam," Abba yace "ah hassan anyi aure kuma shiru ake jinka" 🤣

Ammu yace "a a Babu komai, yaya dama ita maimunan ce tasamu Juna biyu, yana Gama fadar haka ya kashe wayar" 🤣

Haka yayi ta farinciki aranar

"Sis kisa wannan ma may be zaki buqata," 

"kai barshi anty husna, wannan kayan ma sun isa," 

"tokisa books dinki, karki zauna kina tunani gara kina dubawa ko?" 

"Ok" nace nakarba nasaka acikin jaka, tsaf nagama hada komai 

Abba zai wuce school yabani dubu goma yace in rike, koda abinda zan buqata, mama ma tabani kayan miya da komai tace na kaiwa kala, husna kuma tace idan naje na mintsini kaka 🤣

Su dukansu ne suka kawoni inda zan hau mota, hijabin dana zirma ajikina shine ya boye abinda yake tare Dani 

Saida motar mu ta tashi, sannan su Abba suka dawo gida, shikuma ya wuce mkrnta daga nan 

Anty maimuna anata fama da laulayi, danma husna tana Zuwa wani lokacin tana taimaka Mata, ga kuma mama da itama kusan kullum batajin dadin jikinta 



   **   ***   **



Ina hanya araina nace, Allah sarki su mama koyaya suka koma gida, abinda na tuna ne yasa natura hannu na cikin jakata da sauri 
Wayata na dauko wadda ni kaina namanta lokacin dana kashe ta, so nake kawai inji muryar su mama da husna 

Ina kunnata tafara hooking, text ne suke ta shigowa ba ma adadi, wasu na qawayen mu ne wasu na Zeey Akan tayi missing dina, saina Atu tana fadamin takirani bata sameni ba, Amma tanamin albishir Akan ta haihu tasamu mace, murmushi kawai nakeyi, text mafi yawan kuwa daga ahmad sai saudat 

Kuka nafara lokacin dana karanta na saudat 

"sis mene duniyar kuma? Kin wani tafi zaria kin share ni "

" sister feenah wallahi nayi kewar ki, wai kinsan ma meya faru, wallahi daddy yayimin miji "

" sis feenah yasu mama Allah yasa de lafiya Bama samunki, dan'uwana yana cikin tashin hankali feenah, har kuka yi yake a dalilin ki "

" sister andaura aurena Yau, shikkenan kinji dadi tunda kinsan banda hanyar dazan sameki balle in fada miki "

" sister sunan mijina faruoq, yana sona sosai, mun tafi Abuja, kema ina muku fatan kasan cewa tare keda yayana "

Ire irin wannan text din saudat taita turomin, nagama karantasu ina hawaye 

My ahmad, shine sunan da na ambata araina, text dinsa rututu, harma narasa wanne zan Buda 

" my feenah, ina kika shiga ne, Dan girman Allah ki yafemin"

"nafeesa nime laifi ne awajanki, kuma gani na durqusa kafafuna aqasa ki yafemin "

" my feenah, bansan wacce kalma zanyi amfani da itaba wajan neman yafiyarki, ki yanke min kowanne hukuncin da kikaga ya dace Dani "

Ina karanta wannan afili na furta zakaga abinda ya dace dakai kuwa, na goge hawayena 

" Haba yarinya tun dazu sai kuka kike, haba ni wallahi kinsa duk jikina yayi sanyi, ni natsani kuka wallahi, ai sai kisa muyi zatan mutuwa zamuyi ahanya, a a, muka sani koke alama kika gani" 🤣 

Wata tsohuwa ce a kusa da feenah take wannan mgnr, 
Ban kula taba kawai nagoge hawayena, 
Su mama nakira, saida naji muryarsu hankali na ya kwanta, ina sauke wayar a kunne na, kiransa yana shigowa 

My ahmad yafuto baro baro a fuskar wayar, yana zaune a office kamar wasa ya gwada number, aikuwa yanaji ta shiga ya miqe tsaye 
My feenah daga mana, yi sauri ki daga Dan Allah, shi kadai yana magana kamar zautacce 

Har wayar tayi ringing ta kare ban dagaba, wani Kiran yasake shigowa, nayi rejected, dana uku ya shigo a fusace na daga, nace 

"waye?" 

Acan bangaren baiyi mamaki ba, yace "feenah," 
nayi shiru 
Yace "Dan Allah kiyi hakuri" 

Nace "hakuri? Ahmad ashe kasan hakuri?" 

Cikin kuka naci gaba dacemasa "ahmad wanne irin hakuri ne ban baka ba Akan ka kyaleni? 
Menayima ahmad? Kasan halin daka sani kuwa? Wallahi nayi DANA SANI Akan kula ka ma danayi tun farko," 

kuka yaci karfina, cikin sheshshekar kuka nace" Dan Allah ka fita daga rayuwata, bana sanka na tsaneka ahmad karka sake nemana, "

nasauke wayar daga kunne na 

" Kayya yarinya kiyi hakuri, haka mazan suke, rayuwar aure da kika ganta sai hakuri,"🤣 

kawai tafara goge hawaye, kallanta nayi da mamaki ni shaf ma namanta tana kusa Dani 

Cikin sauri nayi copy din number's dina suka dawo kan waya, nakarya layin, na wullar dashi ta tagar motar

 yafada yana dora hannunsa Akan kirjinsa, kansa ne yasara yagaza zama, har saida yadora kannasa Akan tebir din dake gabansa 



   **   ***   **



Adedeta na saukeni, naja jakar kayana nayi cikin gidan, kai tsaye bangaren kaka nayi, tana ganina tataho da sauri tazo ta rungumeni, sai kuka, ni kuka ita kuka

Tace" eh yana ciki tun dazu "

. "Subhanallah yallabai, Garin yayi haka?"

   **   ***   **



Naci abinci dakyar, inajin wani daci amaqogarona, dakyar de nadan caccakali abincin, nayi wanka na kwanta, kala ce ta shigo, tace "idan na huta saina tashi nakoma bangaren ta"

ba yanda kala ta iya haka tahakura,

Hawaye naji ya ziraro min nasa hannu na goge wallahi banasan cikin nan, tunda de shima jinin ahmad dinne 
"wallahi bana sanka" 

nafada afili hannu na nadauke daga kan cikin, nasake cewa "nikam bana sanka," 🤣 

wani motsi naji cikin yayi, nace "shikkenan qarasa ni ma kamar yanda shima yanemi kashe ni, ranar nan"

ahmad baice komai ba, ya lumshe idonsa

Tahir yace" alhmdlh jikin da sauqi, dan Allah abokina ka dinga kula dashi nabaka amanar amini na,"🤣

 gyada kansa kawai yayi

[5/6, 3:32 PM] ❣️DANGI DAYA❣️: 🌱DANA SANI 🌱



Writing by Amnah el yaqoub 



Dedicatedg to my family 



30


"Kinyi shiru nafi," 

nace "kaka ai baya nan, yatafi misra," 

" au to shiyasa naji shi shiru, nasan da yasan inda nake aida tuni ya zo" 

Nace "um" 😠

Natashi nayi daki 



   **   ***   **



Akwana atashi Babu wuya awajan Allah, har cikina yacika wata tara, duk kafafuna sun kumbura, nayi wani fari, kaka bata barina inyi aikin komai, ina kwance kokuma in tashi inta zagaya gidan 



   **   ***   **



Baba adamu shine yayi shige da ficensa ya karbo min takarduna, yahada dana wajena yatafi dasu gidansa bansan me zaiyi dasu ba 



   **   ***   **



"Maman nafeesa anya kuwa ba zamuje asbiti ba aduba lafiyarki?
Tunda kika samu cikin nan bakida lafiya Yau fari gobe tsumma, gashi naga watan naki ma ya haura, ga kuma bikin husna dayake ta matsowa saura wata daya da sati daya" 

Mama tace "Babu komai Abban husna, aidama Ana samun hakan, yanzu Babu abinda nakeji,"
Abba yace "Toshiknn" 



   **   ***   **


Su ahmad tunda bikin nan ya matso ake ta zirga zirga kai kace bikinsa ne za'a yi,🤣 baya zama Yau shine can gobe shine can 

Kullum suna shirya yanda bikin zai kasance, 
Haka a bangaren su mama ma, basu zaune basu tsaye, Ammu yayi kokari sosai wajan ganin ya qawatawa yar tasa gidanta, sosai yayi Mata siyaiya ta gani ta fada Babu abinda su mama zasuce sai godiya 



   **   ***   **


"Kaka ni banajin dadin jikina, tun jiya wani irin ruwa nake zubarwa," 

kaka tace "a a nafi to haihuwar ce ta matso inaga zaqi ne, shiyasa banasan wannan ciye ciyan naki," 

tunda kaka ta ambaci haihuwa gabana yafadi, gaskya ina tsoro, qila ma Garin haihuwar na mutu, sai hawaye, kaka ta kalleni tace "to nafi ki adana kukan ki ba yanzu🤣🤣 ba akwai lokacin da zakiyi mai dalili," 

maya finta taja, tace "bari inje in karbo miki rubutu kyasha," bata jira amsa taba tayi waje, na zauna ni kadai na rafka uban tagumi, tashi nayi naje nayi alwala domin gabatar da sallar la'asar 



   **   ***   **



"Wash Allah Abban husna mu tafi asbiti, ina tunanin haihuwa zanyi," 

Abba Gaba daya yarikice, Yama rasa yaya zaiyi 
Husna yakira ta futo da sauri, suka hada duk wani abin buqata sai wani asibitin kudi 
Suna Zuwa kuwa likitoci suka karbeta sai labour room, husna sai kuka take, Abu tun la'asar sai gashi mama takai kusan isha'i bata haihu ba, duk nurse din data futo amsar daya ce bata haihu ba tukunna 



   **   ***   **


Ina zaune kan sallaya na idar da sallah kenan da carbi ahannu na, naji wayar kaka na kuka, kira na qwala Mata, nace " kaka Abba yana kiranki," 

ta futo daga daki ta amsa wayar, sai ji nayi kaka tana salati, 
"ma'azu to anya kuwa Babu wata matsalar ka boye min?" 

"Mama Babu komai wallahi kawai de dama Dan in fada miki ne kisan halin da ake ciki," 

kaka cikin sanyi jiki tace" to Allah yarabasu lafiya"

" amin" Abba yace 

Ta zauna akujera nace       " kaka lafiya kuwa,?" 

Cikin jimami tafadamin abinda yake faruwa gabana naji yabada dam, araina nace nima fa inakan hanya, bayana naji ya riqe da sauri nasa hannu na awajan, nace    " wash Allah, kaka bayana," 

ina yi ina yarfe hannu, kaka tace "subhanallah nafi karde kema haihuwar ce?" 

"Wayyo kaka marata ciwo, zan mutu kaka," 
kaka tace  " tsaya nafi bazaki mutu ba insha Allah," 
Wayarta ta dauko tafadawa baba adamu abinda yake faruwa, yace gashinan Zuwa 

Kala taso ta biyo shi yace ta zauna tunda kaka tana nan, sai ihun kuka nake Mata ina yarfe hannu, yana Zuwa muka shiga bayan motar nida kaka, sai ihun kuka nake musu 
Kaka tanaban hakuri, asibitin gwamnati muka fara Zuwa, saide sun shaida mana sun tafi yajin aiki, haka baba adamu yajuya kan motar sai wani asibitin kudi shi kansa ya rikice, yana tausayi na, ga halin danake ciki gani yarinya 

Inata kaina banma kula da sunan asibitin ba nide nasan muna Zuwa wasu Mata suka karbeni aka dorani Akan gado, suka turani Zuwa labor room 



   **   ***   **


10:30 na dare, wata doctor tazo tace "alhaji saide fa kasa hannu anan, dole aiki za'a yi mata, Dan gaskya karfin ta yaqare, bazata iya haihuwa ba, sannan gashi ta manyanta,"

Kalatynmu takira husna tace taje kano, ta huta kafin bikin, ganin haka yasa nima nace zan bita, Babu wanda yahanani, mama tace min ala raka taki gona 🤔

 nafada abaiyane, watace a kusa Dani ta juyo ta kalleni, tace "sannu fa"

Awa uku muka diba muna tafiya takai mu ga Sudan, muna sauka, mukayi yan cike ciken takardu muka futo daga airport din, wakilinmu ne yakawo mana motoci muka shiga, bamu zame ko ina ba sai gidansa, maza kuma yakaisu wani gidan daban

Bamu bar wajan da wuri ba kasancewar saida aka gamawa kowa, sannan muka tafi gida, daga nan kowa yasai kati, yayi mana bayanin yanda zamuyi amfani dashi, ina kwanciya nasa nawa, nakira su mama, husna ihun murna kamar Zata fasamin kunne

ahmad yana juyowa tahir yayi musu selfie, sunyi mugun kyau, daddy dayake zaune a bayan mota shida driver aransa yace wadannan yara saisu, kuda ake jiran ku kune masu photo😃 kallansu yasake yi, kamar su biyu ne angwayan, shima ahmad inda ace tasan tana nan aida an hada anyi musu auren shida abokin nasa

Anty maimuna ce mai cewa karde ki bari komai yafaru🤣🤣 husna tace to my anty 

Duk inda tayi Ana ga amarya 
Haka ta futo taje gidan Ammu, tayiwa su tahir din kwatance sukaje 
Ruwa takawo musu da lemo suka zauna afalo, sannan ta futo domin su gaisa, dama a kace jini daya yafi karfin wasa, ahmad yana ganinta yaji gabansa yafadi 
Duk Dade ba wata kama suke d feenah ba saide kama ta yan'uwan taka, bayan sun gaisa ne tahir yace " husna ga babban abokina aminina kuma dan'uwana ahmad," husna tace "au dama shine?"

 tahir yace "amin,"

tace" toshkn,"

Tace" na'am, "
nace" gaskya ayi video," aikuwa ta matso mukai ta video muna shirmanmu iri iri, ahaka har kowa yagaji

, ahaka har muka kwashe kwana hudu

keba mekyau bace feenah, kece shugabar kyawawan kawai,"

Kuka nake Sosai tamkar raina zai fita, Zahra ta janyoni jikinta tana Dan bubbugamin bayana alamar inyi shiru, ta sunkuyo daidai kunne na, tace "feenah kiyi hakuri kidena kuka, kar hankalin mutane yadawo kanki kuma yanzu"

Babu abinda nake sai aikin tunani

Dasauri wani driver yazo yatemaka masa, yakai shi wajan motarsa, Gaba daya baya cikin hayyacinsa

" hello my son"

Bayan sun dawo gida, dakinsa suka kaishi, aka hada masa shayi, yasha tare da magani, bara'ah ta rufa masa bargo tafita

" amin "

Tahir yace "ahmad wanne irin aure ne wannan?" 
Ahmad yace " tahir mubar maganar nan Dan Allah" 

Tahir yace "ok, to yanzu yaushe zamuje wajan yarinyar sai muji wanne tsare tsare ne yakamata ayi," ahmad yace" Babu inda zani tahir, aide so suke su ganni da auren ko, to ayi auren, Amma maganar gaskya banajin zan iya San wata bayan feenah "
Tahir yace" a a kadena fadar haka "


   **   ***   **


"Mom nafada miki Babu abinda zanyi, haka kawai dad baiyi shawara daniba zai tashi yabadani saikace wata mage" 
Mom ta dafa kafadar ta tace "
LAILA 
meyasa wani lokaci bakya tunani ne, yanzu ashekarunki baici ace kinada yara ba? 
Nide abinda nakeso dake shine kiyiwa mahaifinki biyayya, inba haka ba,tun baya fishi dake to zai fara yanzu" 
Laila tace "mom nikam banasan wannan auren, nafisan rayuwa ta haka, mom bansan fa wanda zan aura ba, saboda me dad zaimin haka?" 
Dafa kafadarta hajiya salaha tayi, tace "laila zaki auri yaro, yaron ma me tashe, me facaka da nera, kuma yaro danye domin kuwa kin girmeshi" , laila tace "mom kamarya? Me kike nufi?" 
"Dan gidan alhaji muhd marzuk zaki aura laila, ko banza zaki zauna cikin daula kiyi yanda kikeso da kudi laila, ki hau motar da kikeso" 


**   ***   **

"Momy gaskya ankon nan yayi kyau, ina farin cikin auren nan na yaya, Amma zan so ace da qawata feenah akayi," Momy tace "saudat muma munso hakan Kodan farin cikin my son 
Amma toya za'ai, haka Allah ya qaddara," 
saudat tace "hakane, Allah yabasu zaman lafiya," mamy hakima datake shigowa Dakin tace "amin" , tace "saudat abinci yazama ready, yakamata kije ki zubawa farouq din ko, kada ace munyi baqo mun barshi da yunwa" 
Saudat tace "ai mamy Dakin barshi ma nayi masa da kaina, Dan bayasan abincin masu aiki," mamy hakima ta kalli Momy, suka hada ido, mamy hakima tace
" um lalle zamani sannu saudat" 
Shiri suke sosai yan wannan gidan, kowa yana walwala saide uban gayyar baya maraba da wannan aure 


   **   ***   **


Kan gadonta tafada, tasaki ihun murna tace, "cikin sauri haka? 
Abinda na Dade ina nema ne Yau yazo har gida yasameni, ta shege da wata uwar daria 
Tace ahmad zanyi yanda nakeso dakai, tunda kazama mallaki na, dana Gama da mangoro zan yarda qwallansa in huta da quda 


   **   ***   **


Rana bata qarya 

Ayau ne dubban mutane suka shaida daurin auren ahmad muhd marzuk, da laila bukar 
Wanda akai Akan sadaki naira dubu dari, lakadan ba ajalan ba 
Ana daura auren ahmad ya sulale Yabar wajan, tahir ne mai kula da komai 
Haka kuma har wannan lokacin shide bai nemi laila ba, bayama ta kanta, dakinsa yashiga ya kulle yaita kuka shi dakai (toya zakai Malam amadi) 


   **   ***   **


"Mom ni narasa gane wannan wanne irin aure ne, angonfa har yanzu bai zoba, sannan akwai dinner da qawayena suka shirya shima kuma naga alamar bazai zoba, tunda nima bansamu matsayin dayazo yaganni ba," 

Hajia salaha tace "laila 'yata yi hakuri bari nakira step momynsa" 
Mamy hakima ta kira tayimata bayanin komai, nan mamy hakima tace
" karta damu aci Gaba da hada komai insha Allah zaizo" 

Nanfa su laila aka fara shiri baji bagani 
Tun magrib aka fara ta ruwa awajan dinner, ango de bayan Mamy hakima tayi masa fada, saida tahir yadage sannan yayarda suka tafi 

Amarya ta Dade awajan itada qawayanta, tun daga nesa data hangoshi ta shagala akallansa, yana Nan yanda yake, sai kyau da haske da kuma hutu dasuka sake baiyana atare dashi, daga kallo daya bai sake yi Mata na biyu ba, yana qarasowa tazo ta rike hannun sa sai kwarkwasa take, aikuwa nan masu pictures suka ce in bakayi ban waje🤣 

Tahir ma yafara yi musu pictures, laila sai wani kanannadewa take ajikinsa, shide tahir yanata musu pic, azahiri shide yarinyar bata wani kwanta masaba, Amma Babu yanda zasuyi da hukuncin Allah 
Ahmad yajuyo yace Mata "kewai miye haka eye?" 
Daidai lokacin daya juyo kuwa tahir yayi musu pictures sai ka dauka wasu zunzurutun masoya ne 🤣

Kusa da ahmad yaje daidai kunnansa yace, "abokina bani aron wayarka mana" 

Ahmad ba tare da tunanin komai ba yabashi, yace "haba ahmad kadan saki ranka mana," yajuya yakoma wajan zamansa 

Yana karbar wayar ahmad whatsApp dinsa yahau yayi posting pictures din ahmad da laila, a status, aikuwa nan da nan mutane suke masa fatan alkhairi, yanmata kuma suke DANA SANI da cizon hakori, daga baya kuma ya koma Facebook nanma yayi posting cewa nayi aure ina buqatar addu'ar ku fans 
Sannan yadora pictures din guda biyu, aransa yace nasan ahmad zaiyi fishi Dani Amma zai dawo yahuce, yaya za'ai ace mutum yayi aure Amma kace Babu wanda zai sani 

Dahaka aka Gama dinner din 
Washe gari ne ranar kaiwa amarya, alhaji bukar ya yiwa laila fada sosai Akan tabi mijin ta, saide ita aranta bahaka bane, 
Hajia salaha kuwa, ta gargadeta Akan kada ta kuskura tayi aikin wahala, laila tace "haba mom kamar baki sanni ba, nifa mom banajin auren nan zai wuce 3 months, gaskya dana samu abinda nakeso,kuma nagama samun kudi, to zan kashe auren ne indawo gida, daga baya dad saiya yafemin" 
Hajia salaha tace "lalle sai Yau na tabbatar bakida wayo laila 
Daki Debi kudi kidawo gida, bagara ki zauna kisamu ciki ba, daga bisani kibar masa gidan,saimusa agama dashi , kinga yayinda aka raba gado kinada tarin dukiya mai yawa" 
Laila tace "wow mom banyi wannan tunanin ba" 
Hajia salaha tace "toki kula de yata" 
Dahaka aka dauki laila aka kaita gidansu ahmad cikin part dinsa dayasha gyara 


   **   ***   **


After one week, tunda akai auren ahmad bai taba takawa wajan ta ba, dasafiya tayi yake fita, bazai dawoba sai can dare yayi sosai,🤔 laila abin duniya duk ya da meta, daga baya ne ta kira mom dinta, nan tabata shawara 


   **   ***   **


Yau da safe nagama karatu na, ina kwance ina chting, kamar wasa nace bari de Yau in duba inga ko ahmad yahau Facebook dinsa 

Hawana keda wuya, naje nayi searching sunansa, kai tsaye wajan photos na shiga, abinda nagani ne yayi mugun dagamin hankali 

Afili na furta, "ahmad! Ahmad!! 
Aure ahmad, sai kuka," nan danan gabana yafara faduwa, nace "na shiga uku 
, ahmad yayi aure, ahmad da gaske dama aure zakayi ka kyaleni?" 
Ihun kuka nasa nayi wulli da wayar, wannan ihun ne yajanyo hankalin Zahra da dakinta yake kusa da nawa 
da gudu ta shigo Dakin tace "feenah lafiya?" 
Ban iya bata amsa ba, sai kuka nake 
Ta cacimeni tace "menene wai feenah kiyimin bayani mana" 
Wayata na nuna Mata cikin sheshshekar kuka, nama kasa magana nace Mata "Ah.. Ahm... Ahmad ne" 

Zahra da sauri taduba wayar, nan take tafara salati 

Cikin kuka nace "Zahra kingani ko? , yanzu kin yarda yaudarata yake ko Zahra" 
Zahra ita kanta hawaye take, nace "Zahra...." 
Kafin nayi magana naji qirjina ya rike, durqusawa nayi na dafe qirjina da hannu na 
Dif numfashi na ya dauke 





Mrs Usman ce 🖊

Haka na wuni ranar cikin sa'adah 

After 2 days 
Waya nake ina cikin farin ciki, nida husna, ni kaina har mamakin kaina nake yanda nake cikin farin ciki, kuma nasan hakan baya rasa nasaba da shawarar da Zahra tabani, "anty husna wai kin manta ne?"

tafada tana rufe bakinta da hannun ta

Alhaji bukar yace "hakane alhaji" 

Daddy yace "nima Dan wajena, yafada matsala a dalilin soyaiya, mazai hana mu hadasu aure, nasan insha Allah zasu so junan su sosai," alhaji bukar yace" Amma alhaji kana ganin hakan babu matsala? Yarinyar nan tawafa bataji "
Daddy yace" insha Allah Babu matsala alhaji, muyi musu fatan alkhairi, inka samu lokaci kayimin magana Asa lokacin auren su kawai "

Yan company sun hana yan gidansu suje su dauko shi, sunce zasuje, haka kuwa akayi, su suka tafi, cikin su harda tahir, jirginsu na sauka aka taroshi kamar wani gwamna

Gaskya akwai abubuwan dasuke faruwa," 
da wannan tunanin yatashi yayi wanka, ya kwanta domin yahuta

Gaban Zahra ya yanke yafadi, tashiga uku, karde yar mutane ta mutu ahannun ta, 😳jijjagata tashiga yi tana fadin "feenah! Feenah!! Na shiga uku na feenah Dan Allah ki tashi" 

Dataga kukan bazai kaimata ba, da gudu tayi Dakin abokan zaman su, suma suna zaune suna fira 
Su kaga Zahra tashigo bagatatan 
Cikin kuka tace musu
" feenah, wallahi Babu feenah ta mutu," 🙆🏻

"Ta mutu? !!!" 🙆🏻
Suka hada baki wajan fadar hakan 
Janyosu tayi sukayo dakina, basuyi wata wataba, daya daga cikin su ta fita ta kira musu adedeta, su kuma suka dauko ta, sauqinsu daya batada wani nauyi 🤣 

Wani asbiti naga sun nufa  maisuna mustashfa sheik 
Suna Zuwa kafin ma ayi wani cike ciken kati suka karbeta, ganin yan'uwanta suna kuka 
Saida aka karbeta ne su Zahra suka fara siyan kati da abinda yakamata, feenah kuwa suna Zuwa aka saka Mata abin nan mai temakon janyo numfashi Wato oxygen 

   **   ***   **


Riga ce a jikinta, marar hannu rabin kirjin nan duk awaje, ta feshe jikinta da turare, Tsawon rigar Gaba daya iya rabin cinyarta ta tsaya 
Yau de za'a yi ta taqare 
Wayarta ta janyo, ta kira sofy 
Safiyya tace "a a kaga amarya, ashe Ana jinku, saida kika Gama amarcin zaki nemi mutane" 
Laila tace "hmm kede qawata bari, abin duniya duk ya dameni, sofy ahmad baya zama, duk zulamata kikaga de nakai wannan lokacin Babu abinda yafaru ai kinsan nayi qoqari ko" 
Sofy tace "haba laila, karki bani kunya mana, kamar ba lailan dana saniba, ni wallahi harkinsa naji kunya, inda nice aida tuni anwuce wajan wallahi

Haba laila mai amfanin kissar mu ta Mata, inba haka ba, me amfanin qwaya, kawai ki dura masa yasha, kuyi komai cikin nishadi, balle ma ke dakika Dade kina mararin abun "
Laila ta numfasa tace
" kede bari sofy, baya dawowa da wuri, idan kinga yadawo to yafaki idona yaga nayi bacci,🤣 shiyasa nima Yau nace komin dare ina nan ina jiran sa, idan yasan wata baisan wataba "



   **   ***   **


Tsawon lokaci nadauka sannan numfashi na yadawo normal, ina kwance abin duniya yadameni, saide idona yana rufe, araina nace

" my ahmad, ashe bazaka zama mallakina ba, 😭nikam nayi DANA SANI Akan yin soyaiya ma baki dayanta" 
Salma ce daya daga cikin wanda suka kawoni, tace 
"
Zahra to ki kula da ita zamuje gida ko abinci ne akawo" 
Zahra tace "a a ai likita yace sha biyu ma zai iya sallamarmu, ba'a kwana a asbitin" 
Salma tace "to bari muje de" 
Taja hannun yar'uwarta suka tafi 

Basu Dade da fita ba, likitan yazo yace zamu iya tafiya ma kowanne lokaci, 
Zahra ta amsa masa 
Bude idona nayi wanda yayi jajir saboda kuka, nace "Zahra Tome muke jira kawai mutafi, Babu abinda yakemin ciwo yanzu" 
Zahra tace "a a mujira su Salma su dawo saimu tafi baki daya" 
Komawa nayi na kwanta, Zahra tace "feenah kiyi hakuri, haka Allah ya qaddara mana 
Banyi tunanin ni kaina zai iyayin haka ba 
Amma ki dauki komai a matsayin kaddara, kicire ahmad aranki," 
Cikin sauri na tari numfashin ta, nace
" bazan iyaba, bazan iyaba Zahra" 🤣 

Zahra tayi murmushi tadawo kusa Dani ta zauna tace "feenah dole kicire ahmad aranki, Kodan lafiyarki" 
Duba nan 
Ta miqomin katin asbiti na, kallan katin nayi ba tare dana karba ba, nace
 Mata "menene?" 
Tace "hawan jini feenah 
Likita yace kinada hawan jini, 
Kinga kuwa yakamata kicireshi aranki Kodan lafiyar ki feenah, nafi kowa sanki da ahmad feenah, Amma tunda kika ji nafadi haka, banasan ciwon so yasa kikamu da wani ciwon da hakan zai taba lafiyar ki, tun kina yarinya "

Tashi nayi zaune, nakamo hannun ta, na rike, nace"Zahra, nasan bazaki bani shawara marar kyau ba, insha Allah zanyi amfani da shawararki, Amma maganar incire shi araina Gaba daya, bazai iyuba, zande kamanta 
Zahra nayi tunanin likita zai fadi wani babban ciwo akaina, Amma hawan jini, najima da Sanin ina dashi,duk a dalilin ahmad duk da likita bai fadamin ba, "

Zahra ta rungume ta, tana bubbuga bayanta alamar rarrashi 

Nace" Zahra, nagama lura mukhtar yana sona, kawai zan bashi dama, muyi soyayyar mu, idan ma auren ne, kawai zan aureshi Zahra "
Zahra tace" hakane feenah, bazan hanaki ba, Allah ya tabbatar muku da alkhairi "

Su Salma ne suka shigo da leda ahannun su, sin siyomin kayan marmari, Allah sarki 
Anan Zahra tafada musu abinda likita yace, nan suka fara hada yanasu yanasu 


  **   ***   **


Tanajin dirin motar sa ta tashi aka gyara gashin attached din data dora akanta 

Bakin qofar Takoma ta tsaya 
, agajiye yadawo gida Yau, yana sanye da farar Riga, baqin wando, sai yar saman itama baqa, Amma ahannun sa yariqeta 
Sai wayoyinsa dasuke hannun sa 
Yana shigowa hankalin sa kwance 
Kawai yaji yayi Karo da mutum, da sauri ya kalleta ya kawar da kai, yace "ke miye haka?" 
Bata bashi amsaba kawai ta rungume shi, Ahmad yace "a a ke cikani mana, wanne irin shirme ne kikeyi haka?" 
Laila fara shafashi , cikin qissa tace" haba ahmad, meyasa kakemin hakane? 
Please kagane mana "😉
Ahmad tureta yayi ya wuce 
Dagudu taje ta rungume shi tabaya, tana shafa kirjinsa da hannun ta, daga baya kuma abubuwan nan guda biyu suna mintsininsa 
Jiki da jini, nan da nan yanayinsa yafara sauyawa, laila dataga tarkon ta, yafara aiki, nan da nan, tafara kissing dinsa, Gaba daya ahmad yafara fita hayyacinsa, cikin rada tace "masa my ahmad muje daki please 

"my ahmad "

Ya maimaita sunan aransa, inaaaa bazai iyuba, mace dayace ta dace da Kiran wannan sunan, wato feenah 

"Keeee kirabu Dani nace" 😠

Yafadawa laila hakan cikin tsawa, yashige dakinsa gamida rufowa 
Dafe mararsa yayi da hannayensa, innalillahi me yake shirin faruwa dashi ne 


   **   ***   **


Waya tace tadau qara, ina dubawa naga mukhtar ne, nadauka muka gaisa, Zahra tana gefena a zaune, tana kallo na, saida muka muka Gama gaisawa 
Zahra tace "masoya," kallanta nayi nace "aikuwa munkusa zama hakan 
Danma ban fara bashi kulawar data daceba" 
Zahra tayi tagumi tana kallo na cikin murmushi batace komai ba 


   **   ***   **


After 3 month, tuni mun shiga level 4
Na warware ina mu'amula ta, kamar da, saide ahmad yana Nan daram araina, Amma ina kokarin kawar da hakan araina 

Soyaiya tayi nisa tsakanina da mukhtar, harya kasance baya awa biyu kyakykyawa ba tare daya kiraniba, yanzu haka saura wata hudu ingama karatu na, haka kuma munyi alqawarin aure a tsakanin mu 
Bai taba boye min komai nasaba, saide nice baisan nawa sirrin ba 
Dan haka nace nima inasan magana dashi mu hadu a school gobe kuma ya amsa min, zai jirani a school din Zuwa goben 

Rufe idona nayi, araina nace gara infada masa gaskya tun yanzu, idan yanaga zai iya aure na shikkenan 


   **   ***   **


"Nagaji! Nagaji!! Nagaji!!  Sofy, kawai zansa masa magani yasha ba tare daya saniba komai yafaru cikin salama, yafi ace ina zubar da ajina Akan wannan yaron"

11:45 yadawo gida 
Laila tana labe tana kallansa harya shiga dakinsa yarufo kofar 
Sake fesa turare tayi, tafita ahaka, bata zarce ko'inaba sai dakinsa, tana shiga taga baya nan, motsin danaji a toilet shiya tabbatar Mata da wanka yake 
Zama tayi agefen gadon, tana qara Baza gashin kanta

  da safe Dakin yakoma, yashiga toilet yayi wanka cikin sauri ya shirya cikin farar Riga mai ratsin baqi, sai farin wando, sumar nan tasha gyara sai qamshi yake, wani akwati ya bude, agogo ne aciki kala kala, ajere, yadauki daya yana daurawa cikin sauri,fridge yabude yadau ruwa zaisha, saiya fasa yace kada cikinsa yayi ciwo baici komai ba yafara da ruwan sanyi, maida ruwan yayi, kallan gadon yayi laila tanata bacci, ba sallah bare salati, ahmad yace inkin gaji kya tashi, cikin sauri Yabar part din

 part din su momy

Aikuwa  kamar jira take su Gama magana, sai gata da gudu ta shigo, tana cikin uniform dinta da school bag dinta a bayanta, kayan yayi Mata kyau sosai, tana Zuwa falon taga baqi dayawa, batabi takan kowa ba tayi wajan mama da gudu, ta rungume ta, wata sallamar suka sakeji abayansu, wani yaro ne yashigo yace

saida yafada sannan mama takalli hannun baby, aikuwa yana hannun ta, mama tace
" bashi abinsa kafin na dakeki," baby tace "to wayace shima ya tsokaneni," 

yaron yace "karyane niban tsokanetaba," mama ta karbi abinsa tabashi, tace " baby nahanaki rigima, bakyaji ko," turo Dan qaramin bakinta tayi gaba, tace "to mama nadena" 
Ammu ne yace "baby ba gaisuwa?" 

Wajansa taje suka gaisa, tana juyawa taga husna Takoma wajanta da gudu, feenah tana kallanta, husna tace " baby ga anty Baku gaisaba," 

kallanta ta maida ga feenah, suka zubawa Juna ido, baby sai kallanta take, tasauri Takoma wajanta, tace" ya sunanki? "

Feenah tace" baki sanni ba?"
 Baby tace" daga ina kike? "
Feenah tace" makaranta," baby tace" Amma zaki zauna damu bazaki gudu ba irin na anty husna" 
Feenah tace "eh zan zauna," baby tace

" to Amma... "

Mama tace "baby barta ta huta tagaji banasan tambaya kinji," 

Abinci mukaci  nayi wanka na tafi daki nakwanta, baby ce tashigo Dakin ta tsaya abakin kofa, tace
" sabuwar anty in shigo?" 
Feenah tace " shigo," tana Zuwa tahau jikinta, takama kallanta, feenah tace "baby lafiya? Wannan kallan fa," 

baby tace "ina sanki, 
Nan dinki nake kalla irin nawa," takai hannun ta kan dimple din feenah, 
Feenah tace "um nagani, yanzu bacci zanyi, nagaji, tashi kije wasa," 

baby tace "tonima zanyi baccin," 🤣 

feenah tace "to kwanta kiyi" , kwanciya baby tayi, feenah tana rufe ido bacci ya dauketa, baby tatashi a hankali taci Gaba d kallanta, tanata daria ita kadai 
Matsawa tayi jikinta tashiga jikinta sosai nan itama tayi bacci 

Mama ta kalli husna tace, "kiramin baby nasan tanada home work, yanzu haka wasa take," husna na fita ta duba ko ina babu baby, Dakin feenah tashiga, kawai tagansu sunata bacci, husna tace "mama zokiga" 🤣 

, mama nazuwa tagansu aranta tace Allah sarki, uwa da d'a kenan soyaiyar nan de dole saita wanzu atsakanin su 
Juyawa tayi, tace "ashe bacci suke," tabar husna anan 
Itakuwa husna kallansu take cikin birgewa 

Gab da magrib nafarka, ina juyi naji mutum ajikina, kallanta nayi da mamaki, yanzu yarinyar nan saida ta kwanta ajikina, kallanta nake da mamaki nashafa kanta, Natashi zan shiga wanka, ina motsawa itama tatashi, cikin muryar bacci, tacemin "nima natashi," 🤣 

nace "to jeki wajan mama," tace "wanka zakiyi?" Nace  " eh," 

tace "nima to kiyimin wankan" 
Nace cikin raina Yau nagamu da jaraba,🤔 nace "jeki mama tayi miki," tace "um um ni kece zakimin" , girgiza kai nayi nashiga wanka, na shirya nabarta,dataga zan fita nabarta adakin kawai yarinya tasa kuka,🤣

 tace "Allah saikinmin," kallanta nayi kawai, Babu yanda na iya dole saida nayi Mata wankan
 
da daddare nabawa kowa tsarabarsa yan biyu sai murna suke, kayan su iri daya su ribom su takalma, saina husna itama kaya, sai na Abba da mama kuma, 
Baby cikin sanyin kuka tace" Toni ina nawa?" Feenah tace " ai banda ke" 
 tajuya wajan mama tace
" wai mama banda ni,"

 mama tace "yi shiru mana baby kefa sai ragwanta ko?" 
Feenah tayi daria tace
" toga naki," cikin murna izdihar tace "yeeee" 

Haka ta ishi kowa da surutun kayanta ranar, washe gari su Ammu suka koma suma da tasu tsarabar, tare suka koma da husna, gidan ya rage daga su sai su, da yamma su aysha sukazomin sannu da Zuwa da ita da kala da kaka sai baba Adamu, sun jima sannan daga baya suka koma gida 

Sati na daya agida, ina kwance afalo, ina kallo, dayake da weekend ne Babu school saiga baby ta futo daga daki tace
" sabuwar anty" 
Nace "menene,? Kinfa dameni baby," 

hannun ta ta dora Akan maqogaronta tace "anty kinji zuciyata tauri ne da ita" 🤣 

Na kalleta naga inda hannun ta yake, batama San inane zuciyar ba,🤣

 nadaura hannu na Akan maqogaron nace "um aikuwa zuciyar taki tauri ne da ita kamar ta ubanki" 🙆🏻😳

Mama da futowarta kenan daga daki taji wannan firar tasu, cikin mamaki tace " nafeesa kinada hankali kuwa?"

Dedicated to my family 

43


Baby ma tazaro ido takama bakinta,🙊cikin mamaki tace "da Abba kike?" 
Mama ce taqaraso wajan da sauri, ta dakawa feenah harara, taja hannun baby sukabar wajan 

Dafe kaina nayi da hannu na, oh ya Allah, meyasa na aikata haka? Yakamata indinga daidai ta kaina Akan yarinyar nan 

Tashi nayi natafi daki 

Mama ta kalli izdihar tace "baby, idan anty tana hutawa kidena Zuwa wajan ta kinji?" 
Baby tace "to mama, Amma ina Santa," 

mama tace "naji, naji baby nasan kina Santa, Amma kidena damunta kinji, oya dauki littafin ki, kiyi karatu" 


   **   ***   **


"Gaskya my heart yakamata kazo mana hutu, yanzu fa watanka biyar," tahir yace "maman mima, tunda nayi wata biyar ai kamar yaune zan dawo, ki qara hakuri, yakika ga autar mama?Da fatan tazo lafiya," 

husna tace "lafiya kalau, takusa Zuwa minma," tahir yace "eh gaskiya kam yakamata, Allah yakawo ta lafiya,"

   **   ***   **


"Mama zanje maqota mu gaisa na dawo,"

baby tayi sauri tatashi, tana kade hannunta, zatabi bayan feenah,🤣

 mama tace "zo nan izdihar, karkije aturaki titi da niyya mota ta kashe ki, 😃" 
buta tadauka Zata shiga toilet, feenah ta girgiza kai kawai ta fita, izdihar naganin mama tashiga toilet, tayi wuf tafice da gudu, 🤣
Nayi nisa atafiya, naji anjamin hijabi na, cikin murna tace

Feenah tace" nima inasan tafiya dake, Amma bazai iyu ba, kiyi hakuri kina Zuwa school kuma gasu mama da Abba, da qawayen ki na school,"
cikin murna tace

Nima nace "ina sanki baby," tafiya mukaci Gaba dayi, nace "baby baki gaji bane? Oya zona goyaki," 
tsugunna wa nayi tahau bayana, muka tafi, tace " sabuwar anty, kina goyani kullum, mama bata goyani," 

nace "baby bakisan sunana bane?" 
Tace "emana, ya sunanki?" 
Nace "sunana baby nima, irin naki," 
tace "laaa ashema sunanmu daya," 

nace "keni bansan shirme sunana nafeesa," tace "nide baby zance," 🤣 

nace "kina fada ina bige wannan bakin naki," daria tasa, ahaka mukaje unguwar kafin mudawo tayi bacci, mama na falo taga mundawo, tace
" ashe ta biki," nace "eh mama, ai tayi bacci ma," mama tace  " itama de batada zuciya," 

murmushi nayi kawai na kyale mama 
Naje na kwantar da ita 

Da daddare ina karanta novel awayata, nayi wanka nayi shirin bacci, saiga baby tazo da littafi tace, "sabuwar anty inzo ki koyamin karatu?" Nace

" eh, zo ki zauna," 
ta zauna nafara yi mata, kuma sosai nafahinci yarinyar tanada kokari, nace "to yimin bayani," 

filla filla tafara yimin bayanin, nashagala ina kallanta, har muka Gama, nace "to ki tashi kije ki kwanta," 

matsowa tayi jikina tace
" um um, sai anjima tukun," 🤣 

nace "to karfa kimin bacci mama tazo tanata nemanki," tace "nifa anan zan dinga bacci, nabar Dakin mama," 🤣 

nace "a a baby yahaka," 

tace "Toni wajanki nakeso," tafadi haka kamar zatayi kuka, nace
" toyi shiru, kwanta," 

Maimakon ta kwanta Akan gadon, saita matso jikina ta kwanta, tace
" sabuwar anty," 

nace "baby kidena fadamin sunan nan," 
tace "to, taci Gaba da cewa kema kina sona ko?" 

Kallanta nayi nace," sosai ma baby, ina sonki, kamar cikin duniyar nan baki daya," 

murmushi tasaki bata sake magana ba, bacci yadauketa 

After 1 week 
Nagama shirya kayana tsaf, nace " to mama zan tafi, Abba saina dawo,"

 Abba yace" to mama na idan kinje ki gaida su husna," nace" to zasuji Abba, "
Jakata na dauka, aikuwa baby tasa kuka tariqe jakar
," Allah Abba zan bita, nide bazata tafi tabarni ba,"

 Abba yace" ke izdihar, banasan shirme kinji ko, idan kin bita makarantar fa? "
Nima idona yayi ja, Inason tafiya da ita, acikin raina, to naga su mama basu ce intafi da ita ba, 
Na dago kai nace" to Abba kabarta mutafi," Abba yace" mamana school dinta fa?" Nace
" zan shiga makarantar inyi musu bayani", mama tana kallanmu tace "nide nafeesa karki dauki yar nan kinga babu idonmu ki cutar da ita," 🤣 

nace "mama wallahi Babu abinda zai faru," tace "Toshiknn" 
Aikuwa aka hada mana kayanmu awata jakar, dole Abba ne yace shi zaije school din ya nema Mata alfarma 
Amota haka taita bani labarin school dinsu, gidan husna direct muka sauka, ummi sai nan nan take damu, Gaba daya bangarenta muka dawo, sai dare muke komawa part din husna, washe gari agidan kaka muka wuni, nida baby dasu mima 
Har mijinta tahadani dashi mun gaisa, yace shima yaso yana gida nazo, Zahra ma takawo min ziyara, haka muka wuni muna bawa anty husna firar Sudan 
Munje gidan kala ma, mun wuni, aysha anata shirin biki, mai gyaran jiki tanata fama, wani watan za'a sha biki 

Duk wani wajan yawo da wasan yara babu inda bankai baby ba, muyi ta pictures nida ita, muna wanka zan zare ta mu shiga gari, mune gidan zoo, mune Park, sosai munsha yawo, 
Nima na kaiwa Zahra ziyara nida baby, haka tabawa baby sweet sosai, har su pictures sunyi dasu video sunata shirmansu 
Satin mu daya a kano muka dawo gida 

Cikin sati biyun danayi agida, sosai muka shaqu da baby, bana iya bacci ba tare dana jita ajikina ba, pictures, videos duk haka nake yi Mata da wayata, wani kuma muyi tare, mun shaqu sosai, kuma mun saba, ahaka har sati biyu na tacika, nafara shirin komawa makaranta 
Ranar dazan koma, saida aka yiwa baby wayo tatafi school sannan muka tafi kano, nahadu da Zahra muka  tafi airport, ina kuka mukai sallama dasu mama da husna dasuka rakoni 
Kuka na daya baby, bansan yaya zataji ba idan taga bana nan 

Muna sauka kuwa nakira su mama nace musu mun sauka, tacemin ga baby nan tana zazzabi, tun rana take kuka, taga bana nan, ni kaina kuka nasawa mama, tace" to yanzu kukan zakiyi kokuma so kike nima ki karyamin zuciya, ga yarinya babu lafiya", haka mama taita min fada har mukai sallama 


   **   ***   **

 wani lokacin ina karatu, haka nake bari, sainaji muryar ta sannan zanci Gaba da karatun, Allah Allah nake ingama karatun nan in koma kusa da yata, inde ina missing dinta videos dinta nake kallah kokuma in kalli pictures dinta, haka mukhtar ma, har pictures dinta natura masa

ahmad ya karbi iv din yace "hassan ko itace wadda kadauko daga school kwanaki?" 

Ammu yace "a a ya'uwarta cede," 
ahmad yace "Toshiknn insha Allah zanyi kokari inzo nima," 

daga haka yayi waje 


   **   ***   **


"Izdihar karfa kiyi wani wajan, wanka kawai zanyi inzo mutafi, kinga abbanki yaji ma da shirya wa," 
baby tace "to mama"

Mama nagama komai, suka dau hanya 
Gidan kala suka sauka, anan suka tarda su husna da yan biyunta harda ummi 
Anata hidimar biki, husna na wajan amare anata shirya amarya 🤣

Karfe biyu daidai aka daura auren aysha da angonta nasir,akofar gidansu, 
izdihar na Zuwa wajansu mama da mima, nan suka fara wasa, Ana wasa rimi rimi, anan ta nuna halin nata na tsokana, 🤣🤣
mama da mima suka biyo ta da gudu, suna saisun Rama

 "boyeni, boyeni," 🤣

Tahir yaci gaba dacewa, "Amma hakan bawani matsala bane, idan muka dora shi Akan magani insha Allah za'a samu abinda akeso" 
Ahmad shiru kawai yayi yana kallan tahir 
Laila nagama Jin wannan batun tasaki tsaki, fuuuu ta tashi tafice daga office din har tana bigewa da kujera Amma ko juyowa batayi ba 

Ahmad ya sunkunyar da kansa, tahir  yayi shiru,shi kansa ya rasa da wanne kalma zaiyi amfani da ita wajan bawa aminin nasa hakuri, saboda haka yatashi daga kujerar dayake kai, yadawo ya tsaya agefen ahmad yafara bubbuga bayansa alamun rarrashi,

 ahmad yadago kansa cike da damuwa yace
 "meyasa tahir? Meyasa?" Nan danan qwalla ta cika idonsa

Tahir idansa yakada yayi jajir, yace. "Ahmad kayi hakuri mana,ni dakai duk dayane ahmad, kaga karkasa damuwa aranka, akan abinda yafaru, ni inde haihuwa ce wallahi nahakura ahmad kadauki su mama, nabaka su, inde Akan yara ne zaka daga hankalin ka, nina hakura, banaso ka sake shiga cikin damuwa ahmad, tunda kasan abinda yake damunka, ba'a San kacika tunani "

Ahmad ya kalleshi yace
" tahir tabbas abin da ciwo, Amma Babu komai, damuwa ta daya tahir meyasa baka sanar Dani hakanba tunda farko?" 
Tahir yace" ahmad tayaya zan tabbatar da abinda nake tunani bayan bakada aure alokacin? 
Ni kaina hasashe nake a wancan lokacin 
Ashe abinda nake tunani zai zama gaskiya, kaga yanzu tashi muje mu lallaba madam mu bata hakuri "
Ahmad yace" tahir yi zaman ka, kabarta kawai idan nakoma gidan zamu tattauna nida ita,ni fishinta bai wani dameni ba, saboda bana kaunar ta, ni badan kada nayiwa Allah butulci ba, danace gara ma da bazan iya haihuwar ba, dade inhada jini na da laila,saboda banga tarbiya awajanta ba bare yaran dazamu haifa su samu, sannan tahir kabar maganar su mama, kaimafa su kenan awajanka tahir, ummi tanasan yaran Sosai saboda me ni zan dauke muku su,
Tahir inajin kaunar izdihar sosai araina, banajin iyayen dasuka haifeta ma, zasu kaini sonta 🙆🏻
ni idan aka bani itama ta isheni rayuwa 
Katayani da adduah tahir Allah yasa iyayenta su amince da buqatata "

Tahir yayi ajiyar zuciya, yace" ahmad karka damu inde Akan wannan ne, idan ma basu yarda ba, nima zan shiga cikin maganar insha Allah zasu bamu ita,"

 yaqarashe maganar yana murmushi, ahmad yayi masa wani irin kallo, yace" abamu ko abani? 
Aiku kun nema anhanaku, yanzu lokaci nane, "
tahir yace

ahmad yasaki daria kamar bashida damuwa yace" kai Amma tahir naji dadi wallahi, idan aka bani izdihar wannan uncle din datake kirana dashi denawa zatayi, takirani da.... '' 

Cikin sauri Tahir ya katse shi, yace "daddy?" 
Ahmad yace "no banasan daddy
Nafisan takirani da Abeey," tahir yace "um kun koma larabawa kenan," 

ahmad yace "ka manta da jikansa kake tare, Dan renin hankali kawai," 

tahir yace " naji," 
Ahmad yaci gaba da fadin " idan kuma na auri my feenah, nasan itama inde taga baby Zata sota, kaga ita kuma saita dinga kiranta da ummi" 

Tahir ya harare shi cikin wasa yace "wannan ai sata ce, da gasa" 🤣 

 
Ahmad yace "edin mun sata," 

tahir yazuba masa ido yana kallansa cikin ransa yace Allah yabaka feenah ahmad, wato har yanzu de tana nan aransa, ahmad yace "gaye kallanfa?" 
Tahir yace "kawai so nake naga yaushe zaka haihu, bansan wanne irin so zakayi wa yaranka ba, wannan yarinyar izdihar ma kana Mata irin wannan son"

Ahmad yace "nikuwa me zanyi dashi tahir kawai ganinta zanyi" 
, waya tahir yadauka, yabada umarni, nan da nan aka kawo masa ita,tahir yace "kaganta nan idan mutum yasha ta wuce guda biyu, shine take yi masa illah," 

ahmad yakarba ya karanta, yace "shikkenan nagani ungo abinka

Ahmad yace "no karka damu tahir, nafadama nifa izdihar ta isheni, kabar maganin kawai, latter mayi magana," 

tahir yace "ok," 
yafuto domin rakashi wajan mota, anan yajashi yafara nuna masa yanda yakeso ya maida fasalin hospital din

"Mom wallahi bazan iyaba, dama nafada miki, ai wannan zaman danayi ma nayi hakuri, kinga kuwa yanzu tunda bazai iya haihuwa ba, mezan zauna inyi agidansa," 

hajiya salaha tace
" gaskya kam tayaya ma zaki zauna da mutum fanko, ai gara kibar masa gidan, wannan jaraba har ina, Allah yabaki wani kawai,"

laila taka she wayar ba tare datace amin ba, mai taxi din tabawa umarni yaqara sauri, tagaji da wannan tafiyar dayake kamar bayaso
Tana Zuwa gida batayi bangaren taba, Part din su mamy tanufa, suna zaune dukansu mabruka, yan biyu Muslim da bukhari, da auta maryam, laila tashigo duk bata hayyacinta

" nikuma bazan iya zama da juya ba, Dan haka ku fada masa yasakeni, bazan iya zama babu yara ba, ashe ma Dan naku Dan shaye shaye ne, inba Dan qwaya ba, meya hadashi da shan qwaya har takai shi ga samun matsala, to nide bazan iyaba," 

tana fadar haka tajuya tayi gaba

ahmad najin haka yace cikin ransa sun samu labari kenan 🤣

Mama tace "haba alhaji ita take iko damu kokuma mune muke iko da ita, idan aka bada baby kamar qarin rufin asirinta ne ai, Akan abin kunyar data janyo mana, Dan haka ni aganina kawai aqyaleta," 

Abba yace "to insha Allah idan tadawo sai nayi Mata bayani dakaina," mama tace "hakan yayi," 

Abba Ammu yakira awaya, yafada masa shawarar dasuka yanke Akan maganar ahmad, Ammu cikin ransa yace gaskya ahmad yasamu karbuwa sosai awajan yaya 
Shima kai tsaye yace " ai Babu komai, ahmad kowa yana masa shaidar kirki," 
Mama tace "to ai kaji alhaji, nikam wallahi banji dadi Bama daka bashi sati dayan nan, sai yaga kamar bamu yarda dashi ba, Dan haka kirashi kafada masa gobe yazo yadauke ta su tafi, tunda itama kanta yarinyar tana sanshi," 

Abba yace "hakane, Amma kinsan komai yana buqatar shawara, shiyasa na bashi sati dayan," sake daukar wayar yayi, yakira ahmad, alokacin yana kan hanyar sa ta Zuwa gida, Abba yana fada masa, ahmad ya dinga kwarara godiya, kuma yace insha Allah gobe zaizo gidan, dahaka sukai sallama, parking yayi agefen titi, yafuto daga motar yayi sujud shukur, yanata farin ciki, sannan yakama hanyar gida 


   **   ***   **


Yana Zuwa gidan,tun kafin yagama gyara parking din motar sa laila ta taho da sauri tace,
" dama jiran ka nake, ahmad inaso kasani zamana dakai yaqare, kasakeni kawai shine abinda nake buqata," 

ahmad yace " laila kanki daya kuwa? Meya kawo wannan maganar? Kinga kije ciki, ina Zuwa zanzo sai muyi magana," 
tana huci tayi part dinta 

mabruka naganin ahmad yashigo falon tahada kan qannanta sukai daki, momy tatashi cikin fishi tace" ahmad mai nakeji? "
Ahmad yace cikin rashin damuwa" momy me yake faruwa naganku haka?"
 
Momy ta dauke shi da mari, cikin sauri ya riqe kumatunsa, cikin mamaki yace"momy?" 
Mamy hakima tace   "my son, meya kaika shaye shaye har hakan yahaifar ma da rashin haihuwa?" Ahmad yace "shaye shaye mamy, ni ban taba shaye shaye ba" , 
momy cikin fishi tayi kansa tace "laila karya Zata ma ne? 
Kokuma mune zamuyi Maka karya?" 
Ahmad zuciyar sa takarye, nan da nan yafara kuka, yace "momy ki yarda Dani mana, wannan bahali na bane," 

mamy hakima tace "munji Ahmad Amma me yake faruwa," 

cikin hawaye ahmad yace "momy, mamy, kuyi hakuri, ni ban taba shan qwaya ba, wannan dalilin ne yasa nakori walaya daga cikin gidan nan, wani yabata magani da kudi Akan ta sakamin acikin lemo nasha, kuma tayi nasara akaina, nasha, nasha momy" 

ya qarasa yana kuka 😭 

Yace "wannan qwayar datasamun itace dalilin dayasa likita yace bazan iya haihuwa ba, saboda nasha dayawa, sannan... zaici gaba da magana wani irin Baqin ciki yatokare masa maqoshi, har gobe idan yatuna abinda yayiwa feenah abun yana qona masa rai

Momy tayi jagwab ta zauna Akan kujera, mamy hakima tace "mun shiga uku, meyasa baka fadaba ahmad? Aida an tambayeta waye yasa ta aikata hakan" 


Ahmad yace "mamy na tambayeta harma tabani number datake magana da mutumin, Amma alokacin ina cikin fishi, saina fasa wayar" 

Mamy hakima tace
" innalillahi, kaga tashi kaje kabawa matar ka hakuri, zamuyi magana anjima" 
Ahmad ya share hawayen sa, yatashi yafita 

Yana Zuwa part din, yaga laila bata nan, Dan haka yabita daki, tura kofar yayi a hankali yashiga, Amma kuma waya take bataji shigowarsa ba, taci Gaba da waya tana fadin 

"Wallahi sofy kede bari, ai nake fada miki, qwayar dana bayar asaka masa ne kwanaki, 😳🙆🏻toya zanyi sofy, nakira shi nayi nayi dashi tawaya ina kaunar sa, yabani dama inbaiyana masa son danake masa, Amma yaqi, shiyasa kin sanni ba jira nabawa musa aikin, Naso kawai dana dandani zumar sa in wullar dashi, Amma bansamu hakan ba, Dan haka nayi amfani da yar aikinsu kawai, kuma still shima abun yaci tura, kinga kuwa dana sameshi yanzu sama taka, ai kinga saina kyaleshi kawai, yasakeni kawai, akwai alhazan dasuke jirana "

Idonsa ne ya rufe ruf, yadena gani, kamar makaho haka yajuyo baisan inda yake jefa kafarsa ba, yanabin bango ahaka ya qarasa falo,  kan kujera yafada, yana ambaton 

" innalillahi wa inna ilaihir raju'un 😭 "
